### [66] [TINA+: Probing Residual Visual Knowledge in Unlearned Diffusion Models via Diffusion-Consistent Text-Free Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17747)
*Qianlong Xiang,Miao Zhang,Kun Wang,Haoyu Zhang,Junhui Hou,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TINA+，一种基于扩散一致性的无文本逆向攻击方法，用于从视觉角度探测文本到图像扩散模型中概念擦除方法的残余视觉知识。通过优化式逆向和扩散一致性轨迹正则化，TINA+能够可靠地重建被擦除概念的视觉实例，揭示现有方法往往只是切断文本-图像映射而非真正消除底层视觉知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除和探测方法主要从文本角度出发，只关注文本-图像映射是否被切断，却忽视了对应的视觉知识是否仍然存在。作者希望从纯视觉角度（无文本条件）探测擦除效果，以发现被掩盖的残余视觉知识。

Method: 1) 利用扩散逆向（diffusion inversion）在空文本条件下探测生成轨迹是否能重建已擦除概念的视觉实例；2) 引入优化式逆向（optimization-based inversion）解决标准逆向因放大近似误差导致轨迹恢复不忠实的问题；3) 提出扩散一致性轨迹正则化（Diffusion-Consistent Trajectory Regularization），惩罚偏离期望边际能量演化的虚假轨迹，抑制随机初始化模型也能重建目标概念的假阳性问题。

Result: 在12种擦除方法、4类概念擦除任务和多种模型架构上的实验表明，TINA+能够可靠地通过扩散一致的视觉轨迹探测残余视觉知识。结果提供了更强证据表明现有方法往往通过切断文本-图像链接来模糊概念，而非真正消除底层视觉知识。

Conclusion: 现有概念擦除方法大多只是实现了文本层面的消除，底层视觉知识并未真正被移除。TINA+为评估概念擦除效果提供了新的视觉视角和可靠工具，并揭示了当前方法在安全治理上的局限性。

Abstract: Although text-to-image diffusion models exhibit remarkable generative power, concept erasure techniques are essential for preventing harmful content. Existing adversarial probes evaluate these methods by testing whether erased concepts can still be recovered. However, existing erasure and probe methods remain largely text-centric, focusing on whether the text-to-image mapping is severed while overlooking whether the corresponding visual knowledge remains. To investigate this question from a visual perspective, we leverage diffusion inversion to probe whether a generative trajectory can reconstruct visual instances of an erased concept. Under a null-text condition, standard inversion avoids the textual pathway but amplifies approximation errors, hindering faithful trajectory recovery. To address this challenge, we introduce TINA+, a diffusion-consistent Text-free INversion Attack equipped with optimization-based inversion. We also find that unconstrained diffusion inversion may discover spurious trajectories, even allowing a randomly initialized diffusion model to reconstruct the target concept. Such trajectories may falsely indicate residual visual knowledge. TINA+ therefore introduces Diffusion-Consistent Trajectory Regularization to suppress this failure mode. By penalizing trajectories that fall far below the expected marginal energy evolution of diffusion, TINA+ suppresses spurious inversion paths while preserving its ability to recover erased concepts. Experiments across twelve erasure methods, four concept-erasure tasks, and different model architectures demonstrate that TINA+ reliably probes residual visual knowledge through diffusion-consistent visual trajectories. These results provide stronger evidence that current methods often obscure concepts by severing text-image links rather than eliminating the underlying visual knowledge.

</details>


### [67] [Training with synthetic data for drone detection in thermal imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17799)
*Tanel Liiv,Sander Soodla,Nzamba Bignoumba,Alma M. Liezenga,Toomas Pruuden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向地空(G2A)无人机检测的合成优先训练策略，即在合成场景上预训练并在真实热红外数据上微调。研究发现合成数据能有效初始化目标表征，但真实热红外数据仍不可或缺；少量真实数据即可显著缩小域差距，且数据对齐比模型规模对性能影响更大。特征空间中的语义对齐是性能的最强预测因子，辐射特性（熵和动态范围）也对检测鲁棒性有贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G2A无人机在中波/长波红外(MWIR/LWIR)图像中的检测面临纹理信息少、传感器噪声、热对比度弱以及标注数据稀缺等挑战，需要探索如何利用合成数据克服真实红外数据不足的问题。

Method: 采用"合成优先"训练策略：先在合成场景数据上训练以学习初始目标表征，再在真实红外域数据上进行微调。系统评估了合成数据与真实数据的组合效果，并分析了数据集对齐、模型规模、语义对齐及辐射特性等因素对性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明：(1)合成数据可提供有效初始表征，但真实域热成像数据对可靠部署仍然必需；(2)即使少量真实红外数据也能显著缩小域差距；(3)数据集对齐对性能的影响大于模型规模；(4)特征空间中的语义对齐是模型性能的最强预测因子，熵和动态范围等辐射属性也对检测鲁棒性有贡献。

Conclusion: 这项工作为综合使用合成和真实红外数据实现有效的G2A无人机检测提供了基础，强调了数据对齐策略在合成-真实数据混合训练中的核心重要性。

Abstract: Ground-to-Air (G2A) drone detection in medium- and long-wave infrared (MWIR/LWIR) imagery is challenging due to reduced texture information, sensor noise, weak thermal contrast, and the scarcity of annotated data. This work investigates a synthetic-first training strategy that combines synthetic scene generation with fine-tuning on real data. We show that synthetic data provides an effective basis for learning initial object representations, while real in-domain thermal imagery is still essential for reliable deployment. Even small amounts of real IR data substantially reduce domain gaps. Our experiments indicate that dataset alignment has a stronger impact on performance than model scale. Finally, our analysis of the dataset suggests that semantic alignment in feature space is the strongest predictor of model performance, while radiometric properties such as entropy and dynamic range also contribute to detection robustness. This work provides a foundation for combining synthetic and real IR data for effective G2A drone detection.

</details>


### [68] [Scale Matters: Adaptive Granularity Selection for Cross-Species 3D Plant Organ Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17803)
*Carla Salazar,Lazaros Nalpantidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGS-PlantSeg是一种少样本3D植物器官分割方法，通过自适应粒度选择（Adaptive Granularity Selection）动态调整冻结的Utonia基础模型的特征粒度，以适应不同物种和生长阶段的植物形态差异，在多个数据集上取得88.9%的平均mIoU，超越固定粒度基线2.5个点，且仅需少量标注数据即可与全监督方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D基础模型依赖固定空间粒度提取特征，这在植物表型分析中严重限制了泛化能力，因为不同物种和生长阶段的器官形态与尺寸差异显著。固定粒度无法适应这种多样性，因此需要一种自适应粒度选择的机制。

Method: 提出AGS-PlantSeg方法：(1) 复用冻结的Utonia 3D基础模型作为特征提取器；(2) 引入自适应粒度选择机制，为每个具体植物模型动态挑选最佳的粒度层级；(3) 利用选定粒度提取的优化几何特征，输入轻量级MLP分割头完成器官分割；(4) 采用少样本学习策略，仅需极少量标注数据。

Result: 在PLANesT-3D、Pheno4D和Crops3D三个数据集上进行广泛实验，AGS-PlantSeg显著提升了跨物种泛化能力，取得了88.9%的平均mIoU，比固定粒度基线高出2.5个mIoU点。在仅需极少量标注数据的情况下，性能与全监督的植物专用架构相当。

Conclusion: 自适应粒度选择是解决3D基础模型在植物器官分割中泛化性不足的关键，AGS-PlantSeg证明了冻结基础模型配合动态粒度调整可以在少样本条件下达到接近全监督的效果，为植物表型分析提供了高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent 3D foundation models provide powerful feature representations for point cloud learning by controlling spatial granularity. However, relying on a fixed spatial granularity severely limits generalization in applications like plant phenotyping, where organ morphology and size vary substantially across species and growth stages. To address this, we propose AGS-PlantSeg, a few-shot 3D plant organ segmentation method that leverages the frozen Utonia (arXiv:2603.03283) foundation model combined with Adaptive Granularity Selection. By dynamically selecting the best granularity levels for each specific plant model, our method extracts optimized geometric features for a lightweight MLP segmentation head. Extensive experiments across PLANesT-3D (arXiv:2407.21150), Pheno4D , and Crops3D demonstrate that AGS-PlantSeg significantly improves cross-species generalization, achieving 88.9% average mIoU performance and outperforming fixed-granularity baselines by 2.5 mIoU points. Despite requiring minimal annotated data, our approach is highly competitive with fully supervised, plant-specific architectures.

</details>


### [69] [GenRec: Knowing Where to Reconstruct and Where to Generate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17832)
*Ata Çelen,Jaewoo Jung,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Sunghwan Hong,Michael Niemeyer,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenRec是一个多视角流匹配模型，通过观察掩码将重建（observed区域精确重建）与生成（unobserved区域创意补全）显式分离，在架构、监督和梯度流中直接嵌入这一划分，在多个数据集上取得了重建保真度和感知质量的双重最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式新视角合成方法将所有像素置于统一的损失下，混淆了几何重建（源视图中可见像素）与创意生成（遮挡或视锥外区域）两种本质不同的任务，即使注入场景几何信息也无法清晰区分这两类信号，导致重建像素缺乏保真度、生成区域缺乏多样性。

Method: 1) 基于观察掩码（由源相机和单目深度估计器导出）区分可见与不可见区域；2) 流匹配骨干网络联合去噪所有目标视角的RGB和场景坐标图；3) 像素空间细化阶段恢复可见像素的高频细节；4) 同一掩码同时门控监督信号，防止回归信号污染生成先验。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、DL3DV-10K和Mip-NeRF 360上，单视角外推和双视角插值任务中，GenRec在可见区域取得最佳重建保真度，在不可见区域超越纯生成式基线获得更好的感知质量。

Conclusion: 将重建与生成显式分离到架构、损失和梯度流中是可行的，观察掩码引导的策略能够同时提升重建保真度与生成感知质量，证明该设计在两种视角合成任务中均有效。

Abstract: Generative novel view synthesis from sparse input images is rarely all reconstruction or all generation: pixels visible in some source view have a unique correct value modulated only by view-dependent shading, while pixels in disocclusions or beyond the captured volume admit a distribution of plausible completions. Existing generative novel-view-synthesis methods conflate these regimes under a single uniform loss, blurring the line between geometric fidelity and creative hallucinations even when scene geometry is injected through warped point clouds or projected depth. We introduce GenRec, a multi-view flow matching model that builds the reconstruction--generation split directly into its architecture, supervision, and gradient flow. Guided by an observation mask derived from the source cameras and a monocular depth estimator, a flow matching backbone jointly denoises RGB and scene-coordinate maps across all target views, while a pixel-space refinement stage restores high-frequency detail on observed pixels; the same mask gates supervision so regression signals do not contaminate the generative prior. Across RealEstate10K, DL3DV-10K, and Mip-NeRF~360, in both single-view extrapolation and two-view interpolation, GenRec attains the best reconstruction fidelity in observed regions while also surpassing purely generative baselines on perceptual quality in unobserved ones, showing the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [70] [DistillPath: An Efficient 22M Distilled Pathology Encoder Approaching Large Foundation Model Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17872)
*Ramon Kaspar,Andrey Ignatov,Valentina Boeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DistillPath-KS16，通过从大规模病理学基础模型（86M到1.1B参数）中进行知识蒸馏，将22M参数的kaiko ViT-S/16编码器性能大幅提升。仅需教师模型的最终类令牌和补丁令牌，在6000张公开切片上训练，就能在EVA、HEST、PLISM三个基准上全面超越baseline，在EVA七任务平均指标上达到0.795，与1.1B参数的Virchow2仅差0.015分，但参数少约29倍、运行速度快25倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度病理切片编码器通常是数十亿参数的基础模型，在普通硬件上编码和存储数千个切片图块成本高昂。需要紧凑编码器在保持下游性能的同时降低计算和存储成本。

Method: 从已发布的大规模病理编码器作为冻结教师进行知识蒸馏，训练22M参数的kaiko ViT-S/16学生模型。蒸馏食谱仅使用教师的最终类令牌和补丁令牌，在6000个公共切片上训练，无需DINO/iBOT预训练头或大规模数据语料库。

Result: 4个教师模型（86M到1.1B参数）蒸馏后的变体均在EVA、HEST、PLISM三个基准上优于kaiko基线。最强教师选择因任务而异。EVA七任务均值上，DistillPath-KS16-Virchow2达到0.795，距Virchow2仅0.015分，参数少约29倍，运行速度快25倍以上，且在聚合指标上超过H0-mini和GPFM。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏能有效将大规模病理基础模型的能力压缩到紧凑编码器中，实现接近原模型性能的同时大幅降低参数和计算成本。该食谱通用性强，适用于任何暴露骨干令牌的已发布编码器。

Abstract: Many high-performing pathology tile encoders are now foundation models with hundreds of millions to over a billion parameters. Encoding and storing the thousands of tiles in each whole-slide image with such models is costly on commodity hardware, so compact encoders that retain useful downstream performance are a valuable alternative. We present DistillPath-KS16, which starts from the existing 22M kaiko ViT-S/16 encoder and improves it by distilling from released pathology encoders used as frozen teachers. The recipe reads only the teachers' final class and patch tokens and trains on 6,000 public slides, needing neither their DINO nor iBOT pretraining heads nor a billion-tile corpus, so it applies to any released encoder that exposes backbone tokens. We distill four teachers spanning 86M to 1.1B parameters into the same student. Every variant improves the kaiko baseline on all three benchmarks we use, EVA, HEST, and PLISM, and the strongest teacher is task-dependent. On the seven-task EVA mean, DistillPath-KS16-Virchow2 reaches $0.795$, within $0.015$ points of Virchow2, the top-scoring model in our evaluation, at about $29\times$ fewer parameters; it also scores above H0-mini and GPFM on this aggregate metric, though that advantage is task-concentrated rather than uniform. Because it remains a 22M ViT-S/16 with 384-dimensional features, DistillPath-KS16 runs more than $25\times$ faster than Virchow2. Code is available at https://github.com/RamonKaspar/DistillPath, and released model weights are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/RamonK/distillpath.

</details>


### [71] [Improving Complex Moiré Removal with Generative Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17883)
*Xinyang Gu,Zhilu Zhang,Honglei Xu,Yanting Mei,Yukang Ding,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的数据引擎，通过生成式监督来提升复杂摩尔纹去除效果。该方法收集真实世界中含复杂摩尔纹的图像，定位屏幕区域，使用多个图像条件生成基础模型产生候选参考图像，并通过补丁级质量控制筛选最优结果，构建了包含6.8K训练对和约250对测试集的WildMoiré数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像去摩尔纹数据集难以涵盖真实世界中不控制的复杂摩尔纹模式（大规模、多色彩），且这些图案常出现在难以获取干净对应图像的场景中（如公共显示屏拍摄或在线资源），限制了学习型去摩尔纹模型的性能。

Method: 提出WildMoiré数据引擎：(1) 收集真实世界中含复杂摩尔纹的图像并定位屏幕区域；(2) 部署多个图像条件生成基础模型产生候选参考图像；(3) 对候选进行补丁级质量控制和筛选，建立可靠的监督信号，从而构建训练数据集。

Result: 构建了包含6.8K摩尔纹-真值训练对的WildMoiré数据集，以及约250对带有真实干净真值的独立测试集。在ESDNet、SDXL和Qwen-Image-Edit上的广泛实验表明，所提出的生成式监督持续提升了复杂摩尔纹去除的性能。

Conclusion: 通过生成式监督和系统化的数据引擎范式，可以有效构建大规模高质量的去摩尔纹训练数据，为复杂真实世界摩尔纹去除任务提供可靠的数据支撑和方法改进。

Abstract: The availability of high-quality paired data is essential for training learning-based image demoiréing models. However, it remains challenging for existing datasets to encompass the complex moiré patterns captured in uncontrolled real-world scenarios. Such degradations typically manifest as large-scale, multicolored moiré patterns. Moreover, these patterns frequently occur in images for which clean counterparts are difficult to obtain, such as photographs acquired from public displays or existing online resources. In this work, we propose a novel data engine designed to improve the removal of complex moiré patterns by generating training supervision. Specifically, we initially collect real-world images containing complex moiré patterns and localize the corresponding screen regions. Multiple image-conditioned generative foundation models are subsequently deployed to produce candidate references. To establish reliable supervision, these candidates are subjected to patch-level quality control to filter and select the optimal results. Based on this systematic paradigm, we construct the WildMoiré dataset, which contains 6.8K moiré-GT training pairs. For evaluation, we additionally build an independent test set comprising $\sim$250 pairs with captured clean ground truth. Extensive experiments on ESDNet, SDXL, and Qwen-Image-Edit demonstrate that the proposed generative supervision consistently improves the performance of complex moiré removal.

</details>


### [72] [CFB-GBM v2.0: An Augmented Longitudinal Dataset for Multi-Modal Glioblastoma Segmentation, Radiomics, and RANO Progression Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17884)
*Alexandre G. Leclercq,Noémie N. Moreau,Hugo Audebert,Andros Nassar,Thomas Cochin,Thomas Leleu,Loïc Le Henaff,Alexis Desmonts,Yoann Poirier,Aurélie Dubru,Laura Guillemette,Pascal Lecoeur,Kévin Lemasson,Cyril Jaudet,Sébastien Bougleux,Romain Hérault,Carole Brunaud,Samuel Valable,Dinu Stefan,Charlotte Raboutet,Alain Batalla,Joëlle Lacroix,Roman Rouzier,Aurélien Corroyer-Dulmont*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CFB-GBM v2.0数据集的扩展版本，该数据集包含264例接受标准Stupp方案治疗的胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）患者。主要贡献是将跨时间点（t0, t1, t2）的肿瘤总体积（GTV）勾画完成率从35%提升至97%，并基于这些纵向注释导出了RANO 2.0响应类别标签，同时提供了HD-BET脑掩膜、PyRadiomics放射组学特征及WHO分类指南信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶质母细胞瘤是最具侵袭性的成人原发性脑肿瘤，中位生存期仅15个月。开发可复现的计算方法用于治疗响应预测、疾病进展建模和个性化医疗，需要包含纵向、多模态影像数据及全面临床和治疗信息的标准化数据集。此前发布的CFB-GBM数据集存在GTV勾画覆盖不完整的问题（仅35%），限制了其在纵向分析中的应用。

Method: 使用在BraTS 2021上预训练并在CFB-GBM真实轮廓上微调的nnU-Net模型自动生成GTV分割，由五位放射肿瘤学家验证；从纵向GTV注释中导出所有时间点对（t0→t1, t0→t2, t1→t2）的RANO 2.0体积响应类别标签；使用HD-BET计算脑掩膜，使用PyRadiomics提取每个患者时间点和MRI模态的放射组学特征；显式记录每个患者诊断适用的WHO分类指南版本（2016 vs 2021）。

Result: GTV勾画完成率从35%提升至97%，覆盖所有可用时间点。提供了完整的RANO 2.0响应标签、脑掩膜、放射组学特征和WHO分类元数据。

Conclusion: CFB-GBM v2.0作为数据集的重大升级，显著提高了纵向GTV注释的覆盖率，提供了更全面的元数据和预处理工具，将有力支持可复现的GBM治疗响应预测和疾病进展建模研究。数据集已在TCIA上公开可用。

Abstract: Glioblastoma (GBM) is the most aggressive primary brain tumor in adults, with a median overall survival of 15 months. Longitudinal, multi-modal imaging datasets with comprehensive clinical and treatment data are essential to support the development of reproducible computational methods for treatment response prediction, disease progression modelling, and personalized medicine. We present CFB-GBM v2.0, an extension of our previously released CFB-GBM dataset comprising 264 GBM patients treated according to the standard Stupp protocol. The primary contribution of this release is the completion of Gross Tumour Volume (GTV) delineations across all available timepoints ($t_0$, $t_1$ and $t_2$), increasing the overall GTV completion rate from 35% to 97%. This was achieved using a nnU-Net model pre-trained on BraTS 2021 and fine-tuned on CFB-GBM ground-truth contours, with the generated segmentations validated by five radiation oncologists. From these longitudinal GTV annotations, volumetric RANO 2.0 response category labels were derived for all available temporality pairs ($t_0 \rightarrow t_1$, $t_0 \rightarrow t_2$ and $t_1 \rightarrow t_2$). To further ease dataset usability and reproducibility, brain masks computed with HD-BET and pre-computed radiomic features extracted with PyRadiomics are provided for each patient timepoint and MRI modality. Additionally, the WHO classification guideline (2016 vs. 2021) applicable to each patient's diagnosis is now explicitly documented. CFB-GBM v2.0 is publicly available on The Cancer Imaging Archive (TCIA) at https://www.cancerimagingarchive.net/collection/cfb-gbm .

</details>


### [73] [Comparative Study of Out-of-the-Box Technology for Automatic Target Detection and Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17917)
*Alma M. Liezenga,Lotte Nijskens,Henrik R. Baumann,Stefan Becker,Simon Bensberg,Niccolò Camarlinghi,Håvard R. Eiring,Alexander W. Johnsgaard,Tanel Liiv,Giuseppe Martino,Matteo Marturini,Matthias Rapp,Jan Erik van Woerden,Alexander Wolpert,Hugo J. Kuijf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索利用公开可用的模型和民用数据集（如VisDrone）在军事目标检测与识别（ATD/R）任务中取得合理性能。作者在自建军事数据集上基准测试了YOLO系列的六个版本和DETR框架的两种变体，评估了开箱即用版本和VisDrone微调版本。主要发现包括：更大的模型性能更好，DETR模型比YOLO系列更有前景，在域外A2G数据集上微调能改善A2G性能和小目标检测，但所有模型在A2G场景下的小目标检测仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军事ATD/R对决策支持和自主操作至关重要，但公开军事数据集的稀缺限制了目标检测和AI技术在军事领域的应用。作者希望通过探索公开模型和民用数据集来弥补这一数据缺口，验证在缺乏军事域内数据的情况下，这些资源能否提供合理的检测性能。

Method: 作者在自建军事相关数据集上（包含军事车辆、遮挡和小目标等挑战性场景）系统基准测试了六个YOLO迭代版本和两个DETR变体。每个模型评估两种版本：开箱即用版和基于VisDrone数据集（含小目标、空对地视角）的微调版。使用mAP@0.5和mAP@0.5:0.95作为评价指标，从A2G/G2G视角、目标尺寸和模型大小等多个维度进行比较分析。

Result: （1）更大模型性能优于小模型；（2）DETR系列相比YOLO系列展现出更有前景的结果；（3）在域外A2G数据集上微调模型可提升其A2G性能，并对小目标检测有轻微改善；（4）所有模型在A2G场景下的小目标检测仍存在明显困难，表现不佳。

Conclusion: 尽管目标检测技术取得了显著进展，但在军事ATD/R系统中，域内训练数据仍然至关重要。仅依靠公开模型和民用数据集微调，无法完全替代军事特定领域的训练数据，尤其在小目标和空对地检测任务上存在明显性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Automatic Target Detection and Recognition (ATD/R) is critical for military decision support and (semi-)autonomous operations. Recent advances in object detection and artificial intelligence (AI) significantly boosted the potential performance of ATD/R. However, the scarcity of publicly available military datasets limits the application of these systems. As a solution, this paper explores the use of publicly available models and civilian datasets to achieve reasonable performance in military contexts. We benchmark several state-of-the-art models, including six iterations of the YOLO series and two variations on the DETR framework, on a newly acquired military relevant dataset. This dataset features military vehicles and challenging circumstances, including various degrees of occlusions and small targets. The out-of-the-box version of each model is validated alongside a version finetuned on the VisDrone dataset. This dataset features small objects, an Air-to-Ground (A2G) perspective and relevant classes, potentially generalizing to our military ATD/R task. We compare the performance of the models using mAP@0.5 and mAP@0.5:0.95, across A2G and Ground-to-Ground (G2G) perspective, target size and model size, giving insight into the real-time capabilities of models. Our main findings are: (1) bigger models outperform smaller models, (2) DETR-based models show promising results compared to the YOLO series,(3) fine-tuning models on an out-of-domain A2G dataset, improves their A2G performance and slightly improves their performance on small objects, but (4) all models still struggle with detecting small objects in an A2G scenario. We conclude that, despite recent advances in object detection, in-domain training is still crucial for creating capable ATD/R systems.

</details>


### [74] [AppendiGrade: An XAI-Enhanced Deep Learning Framework for Grading Appendicitis in Ultrasound with Gaussian Blur and Grad-CAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17923)
*Fahad Ahammed,Omar Faruq Shikdar,Navid Zaman,Md Tahsin,Md. Nawab Yousuf Ali,Golam Sorwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于超声图像的自动化系统，用于检测和分类复杂阑尾炎（穿孔、脓肿、急性、阑尾粪石和正常五类）。通过对比四种预训练深度学习模型（DenseNet201、InceptionV3、ConvNextTiny、VGG19），并结合图像预处理、超参数调优、模型微调和图像锐化等技术，InceptionV3的准确率从69.21%提升至95.58%。最后利用Grad-CAM生成热力图解释模型预测，便于专家交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂阑尾炎（如穿孔或脓肿形成）与单纯性阑尾炎的准确鉴别是重要的临床挑战。超声作为一种无辐射、安全且经济有效的诊断手段，但其图像解读依赖专家经验，自动化辅助诊断系统具有重要价值。

Method: 使用4679张超声图像（5个类别：穿孔、脓肿、急性、阑尾粪石和正常）训练和测试模型。对比四种预训练深度学习模型（DenseNet201、InceptionV3、ConvNextTiny、VGG19），并应用图像预处理、超参数调优、模型微调和图像锐化等优化技术提升性能，最后使用Grad-CAM生成热力图进行可解释性分析。

Result: 初始配置下InceptionV3准确率为69.21%（第二名），经优化后InceptionV3准确率显著提升至95.58%。Grad-CAM成功生成热力图，标识出模型预测所依赖的感染区域，便于专家交叉核验。

Conclusion: 通过综合优化技术，深度学习模型能够在超声图像上以高准确率自动检测复杂阑尾炎，且Grad-CAM可解释性增强了模型在临床决策中的可信度与实用性。

Abstract: Appendicitis is one of the most common abdominal emergencies worldwide and requires prompt diagnosis and treatment to prevent life-threatening conditions. However, accurately differentiating complicated cases, such as perforation or abscess formation, from uncomplicated appendicitis remains a significant clinical challenge. Among other methods, ultrasound is a safer and more cost-efficient diagnostic technique because of the lack of radiation exposure. In this research, an advanced system capable of automatically detecting complicated appendicitis from ultrasound images was developed. A dataset consisting of 4679 ultrasound images with 5 classes, namely perforated, abscess, acute, appendicolith, and normal, was used for the proposed model training and testing. Four pretrained deep learning models, DenseNet201, InceptionV3, ConvNextTiny, and VGG19, have been employed for detecting and classifying complicated appendicitis. In the initial configuration, InceptionV3 achieved the second highest accuracy, with a value of 69.21%. Owing to suboptimal performance with raw images, further optimization techniques, including image preprocessing, hyperparameter tuning, model fine-tuning, and image sharpening, were applied. These enhancements significantly improved the model's performance, with an accuracy of 95.58% for InceptionV3. The model performance is then explained with gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM), which creates a heatmap of the regions responsible for the model's prediction of the infected areas. This could make crosschecking with experts much easier.

</details>


### [75] [PerFact: Perception-Derived Fact Prompting for 3D Brain MRI Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17926)
*Jianyu Sun,Zhenxuan Zhang,Guang Yang,Peter J. Lally*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"更大的模型或医学预训练骨干是提高放射学报告生成质量的关键"这一默认假设，在3D多序列脑MRI这个体量大、多疾病的场景中发现：模型规模并非关键杠杆，注入提示词中的grounding信息才是决定报告质量的主导因素。作者提出PerFact框架，将上游3D分割和分类的输出序列化为结构化事实句子，注入LoRA适配的视觉语言模型中，实验证明感知派生事实优于检索式先验报告，且预测事实在推理时无需真实标注仍有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告生成几乎完全成熟于2D胸部X光片领域，默认的提升路径是更大的骨干网络或医学数据预训练。作者重新审视这一假设，在更具挑战性的3D多序列脑MRI（体量大、多疾病）场景中验证：模型选择是否真的是报告质量的主导因素？以及什么才是真正的关键控制变量。

Method: 作者提出PerFact框架：(1) 利用上游3D分割和分类模型感知图像；(2) 将感知输出序列化为结构化的"事实句子"（fact sentence）；(3) 将这些事实注入提示词中，交给LoRA适配的视觉语言模型生成报告。研究中固定骨干网络、数据划分、目标报告和适配方式，仅改变注入的grounding来源进行受控对照实验。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 零样本医学/放射学视觉语言模型在脑MRI上迁移效果差，胸片专家模型表现最差；(2) 五种不同规模和家族的骨干网络微调后性能差异极小；(3) 感知派生事实优于检索式先验报告，事实存在时检索变得冗余；(4) 端到端预测的事实无需推理时真实标注仍然有效；(5) 预测事实与oracle事实之间的差距主要源于事实粒度而非生成器；(6) 闭端VQA不损害报告质量。

Conclusion: 在3D脑MRI报告生成中，grounding信息（而非模型选择）是报告质量的主导可控因素。这一发现颠覆了"更大模型=更好报告"的传统认知，为体量大医学影像报告生成提供了新的优化方向：与其追求更大或更专业预训练的模型，不如专注于如何构建和注入更高质量的感知事实信息。

Abstract: Radiology report generation has matured almost entirely on 2D chest radiographs, where the default route to better reports is a larger backbone or a pre-training one on medical data. We revisit that assumption on 3D multi-sequence brain MRI, a volumetric multi-disease regime, and find that the model is not the lever. Zero-shot medical and radiology vision-language models transfer poorly to brain MRI, with chest radiograph specialists failing most conspicuously, and five backbones fine-tuned identically across three model families and an order of magnitude in scale differ only marginally. What determines the quality of the report is the information injected into the prompt. We delegate perception to upstream 3D segmentation and classification, serialize their outputs into a structured fact sentence, and prompt a LoRA-adapted vision-language model with it; we call this \textbf{PerFact}. In a controlled study that fixes the backbone, data split, target reports, and adaptation while varying only the injected grounding, perception-derived facts outperform retrieved prior reports, retrieval becomes redundant once facts are present, and end-to-end predicted facts remain effective without any ground-truth annotation at inference. The residual gap between predicted and oracle facts is explained by the granularity of the facts rather than by the generator. Closed-ended visual question answering comes at no measurable cost to report quality, though the grounding source has little effect on it. On 3D brain MRI, grounding information, not model choice, is the dominant controllable factor in report quality.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Instrument Motion: Recognizing Tissue Tension Toward Surgical Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17935)
*Marko Haralovi,Zhiqi Miao,Alexander Machiel Bont,Jiapan Guo,Frans van Workum,Estefania Talavera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了组织张力识别（tissue tension recognition）这一新的手术视频理解任务，并构建了首个专家标注的组织张力数据集SurgTension，同时提出了基于稀疏点轨迹的轻量级框架TensionTRAC，在腹腔镜和机器人辅助直肠癌手术中实现客观的组织张力识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法主要关注器械分割、手术阶段识别和动作识别，但未能显式捕捉精细的组织处理（tissue handling）这一手术质量的关键指标。传统手术表现评估依赖人工专家审查，耗时且主观，难以规模化。因此需要一个新的任务来客观评估组织张力。

Method: 提出了TensionTRAC框架，基于稀疏点轨迹（sparse point trajectories）建模组织张力，采用紧凑的轨迹编码器（trajectory encoder），无需依赖大规模预训练视频骨干网络。

Result: TensionTRAC在SurgTension基准上取得了与强预训练视频骨干网络相当的竞争性能，同时模型更加轻量。

Conclusion: 组织张力识别是一个有临床意义的新任务，SurgTension数据集为客观组织张力识别提供了基准，TensionTRAC证明了基于稀疏轨迹的轻量级方法在该任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Surgical performance assessment in minimally invasive surgery largely relies on manual expert review, making it time-consuming, subjective, and difficult to scale. While existing surgical video understanding methods address tasks such as instrument segmentation, surgical phase recognition, and action recognition, they do not explicitly capture fine-grained tissue handling, a key indicator of surgical quality. To address this gap, we introduce tissue tension recognition, a new clinically motivated video understanding task for laparoscopic and robot-assisted rectal cancer surgery. To support this task, we construct SurgTension, the first expert-annotated tissue tension dataset, providing a benchmark for objective tissue tension recognition. We further propose TensionTRAC, a lightweight trajectory-based framework that models tissue tension from sparse point trajectories. Using a compact trajectory encoder, TensionTRAC achieves competitive performance against strong pretrained video backbones.

</details>


### [77] [Cross-Domain Generalization in Machine Unlearning via Label-Conditioned Energy Magnitude Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17942)
*Syed Ali Ahmed,Syed Bilal Ahsan,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究机器学习中的"机器遗忘"问题，通过标签条件能量模型（EBM）来直接观察当遗忘某个类别时模型其余部分的变化。提出了一种包含遗忘项、保留锚点、全局边界、能量正则化项和传播项的方法，使得遗忘不仅作用于目标类别，还能通过DINOv2相似度传播到相似样本，同时保持不相关类别的性能。在DomainNet和CIFAR-10上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数机器遗忘方法将遗忘概念视为孤立的，忽略了遗忘一个类别时对模型其余部分的影响。作者希望研究并控制这种"遗忘效应"的扩散，使其既能彻底遗忘目标类别，又能最小化对其他类别的伤害。

Method: 使用标签条件能量模型（EBM）为每个类别分配能量分数。通过提高目标类别图像-标签对的能量实现遗忘，训练目标包含四部分：遗忘项（forget term）、保留锚点（retain anchor to pretrained model）、全局边界（global margin）和能量正则化器（防止能量无限增长）。此外引入传播项（propagation term），根据保留样本与遗忘类别的DINOv2相似度加权应用相同的遗忘信号，使遗忘效果自然扩散到相似图像，而远离的样本不受影响。

Result: 在DomainNet数据集上（涵盖四种视觉域），逐一遗忘老虎、狮子和剪刀类别：遗忘sketch域的类别也能使其从real、clipart和painting域中消除，狮子遗忘误差达98%，剪刀达99%，且效果延伸到最相似的类别。在CIFAR-10上关闭传播项逐一遗忘十个类别：遗忘完整性达100%，其余九个类别平均保留98.5%的未遗忘前准确率。

Conclusion: 通过能量模型框架可以直观地观察和控制遗忘的传播效应。传播项让遗忘自然扩散到相似样本，而关闭传播项时可以实现完全遗忘同时对其他类别影响极小，证明了该方法在遗忘彻底性和模型保留性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific data from a trained model. However, most methods treat the forgotten concept as isolated. In this paper, we study what happens to the rest of the model when a class is forgotten, using a label-conditioned energy-based model (EBM) that assigns per-class energies, making the effect directly observable. We forget a class by raising the energy of its image-label pairs, training with a forget term, a retain anchor to the pretrained model, a global margin, and an energy regularizer that stops the energy magnitudes from growing without limit. A propagation term applies the same forget signal to retain samples, weighted by each sample's DINOv2 similarity to the forget class, so forgetting reaches images that resemble it and leaves the rest untouched. We evaluate on two benchmark datasets: 1) On a subset of DomainNet across four visual domains, we forget tiger, lion, and scissors one at a time. Forgetting a class in the sketch domain also erases it from real, clipart, and painting, with forgetting error reaching 98% and 99% for lion and scissors, and the effect carrying over to the most similar class. 2) On CIFAR-10, we turn off the propagation term and forget each of the ten classes on its own. Forgetting is complete (100%), while the other nine classes retain 98.5% of their pre-unlearning accuracy on average.

</details>


### [78] [SFMformer: A Spatial-Frequency Modulation Transformer for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17966)
*Chih-Hsiang Yang,Chia-Min Lin,Ching-Yu Tsai,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMformer，一种用于轻量级图像超分辨率的稀疏注意力Transformer。核心洞察是稀疏注意力层将"选择质量"和"聚合质量"分离为两个可独立优化的目标，通过在注意力前后分别放置双分支空间增强模块和小波域调制模块来实现。实验表明这两个模块的增益非加性（联合增益超过单独增益之和），在5个基准、3个放大因子的30个PSNR/SSIM指标中28项排名第一，且模型参数量始终低于100万。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力机制已成为轻量级图像超分辨率最有效Transformer的基础，但现有研究未认识到稀疏化改变了网络的改进方式：稠密注意力层只有一处需要关注表示质量（特征聚合），而稀疏层有两处——top-k选择阶段和聚合阶段，被丢弃的token无法在下游恢复。因此选择质量和聚合质量应被视为可分离的优化目标。

Method: 1) 将稀疏注意力层的改进拆分为两个独立目标：选择质量（注意力前模块处理）和聚合质量（注意力后模块处理）。2) 在渐进式聚焦注意力（progressive focused attention）的输入端放置双分支空间增强模块，在输出端放置小波域调制模块，构成SFMformer。3) 采用每块一次而非每层一次的频谱调制策略，将成本降至约六分之一。

Result: 在15个基准尺度对上测量各模块单独及联合效果，发现增益非加性：9对上联合增益超过单独增益之和，且较弱模块的单独贡献与增益差异符号强相关(r=-0.72)——当两模块缓解不同约束时效果叠加，缓解相同约束时效果重叠。SFMformer在5个基准、3个放大因子的30个PSNR/SSIM条目中28项排名第一，所有尺度下参数量低于100万，并在Raspberry Pi 5上验证了实际部署可行性。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力下的超分辨率网络应分别优化token选择与特征聚合两个环节。SFMformer通过前置空间增强和后置频谱调制的配对设计实现了优异性能，且两模块的非加性增益关系揭示了深度学习模块组合的新理解：当模块解决不同瓶颈时效果可复合。同时，该设计在严格资源约束下保持实用（<100万参数、可部署于树莓派），并诚实报告了配对无效的案例。

Abstract: Sparse attention mechanisms, which score all token pairs but propagate only the strongest, now underpin the most efficient Transformers for lightweight image super-resolution. This paper observes that sparsification changes what it means to improve such a network. A dense attention layer has one place where representation quality matters: the aggregation of attended features. A sparse layer has two, because the top-k operator first decides which tokens survive and only then decides what to do with them, and a token discarded at the selection stage cannot be recovered downstream. Selection quality and aggregation quality are therefore separable targets, addressed by modules placed before and after the attention respectively. We test this by pairing a dual-branch spatial enhancement on the input of a progressive focused attention with a wavelet-domain modulation on its output, forming SFMformer. Measuring each module alone and jointly over all fifteen benchmark-scale pairs, we find their gains are not additive: the joint gain exceeds the sum of the individual gains on nine pairs, and the sign of the discrepancy is predicted by how much the weaker module contributes on its own (r = -0.72), so the two compound when they relieve different constraints and overlap when they relieve the same one. Enabling spectral modulation once per block rather than once per layer retains the effect at roughly one-sixth of its cost, keeping the model below one million parameters at every scale. SFMformer ranks first on 28 of 30 PSNR/SSIM entries across five benchmarks and three upscaling factors. We report the cases where the pairing does not help, and deploy the model on a Raspberry Pi 5 to confirm the design is practical under tight resource budgets.

</details>


### [79] [LinCa: Accelerating Diffusion Models via Learnable Decomposed Feature Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17973)
*Jinshan Liu,Haoran Qin,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Jiahui Hu,Zhengan Yan,Yukun Xie,Kerui Shen,Jinkui Ren,Yuqi Lin,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinCa是一个基于可学习可逆网络的特性缓存框架，通过将缓存的特性分解为具有不同连续性的子组件，并为每个组件匹配差异化预测策略，实现扩散模型的高加速比采样，在不到0.2%额外参数下实现5-7倍加速且保持近无损质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像和视频生成中表现出色，但迭代采样的高计算成本是实际部署的关键瓶颈。现有基于特性缓存的训练无需方法采用统一的预测策略，无法适应异构的特性动态变化，在高加速比下导致显著的质量退化。

Method: LinCa通过轻量级可逆网络将缓存特性分解为具有不同连续性质的子组件，对各组件采用匹配其特性的差异化预测顺序。严格的可逆性保证了能无损重建回原始特性空间，形成统一的分解-预测-重建（Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct）流程。此外，针对不同模型和时间步段分别训练预测器，以适应异构的特性动态。

Result: 在FLUX、Qwen-Image和HunyuanVideo上的实验表明，LinCa仅需不到0.2%的额外参数，显著优于现有方法，并在5-7倍加速下保持近无损的生成质量。

Conclusion: LinCa通过可学习可逆网络实现自适应、分组件预测的特性缓存框架，有效解决了统一预测策略难以适配异构特性动态的问题，为扩散模型的高效采样提供了一种高质量、低开销的加速方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image and video generation, yet the high computational cost of iterative sampling remains a critical bottleneck for practical deployment. Feature caching has emerged as a promising acceleration paradigm by reusing or predicting intermediate features across timesteps. However, existing training-free methods apply uniform prediction strategies that cannot adapt to the heterogeneous feature dynamics, causing significant quality degradation under high acceleration ratios. We propose LinCa, a feature caching framework based on learnable invertible networks. LinCa decomposes cached features into sub-components with distinct continuity properties via a lightweight invertible network and applies differentiated prediction orders matched to each component. The strict invertibility guarantees lossless reconstruction back to the original feature space, forming a unified Decompose-Predict-Reconstruct pipeline. By training separate predictors for different models and timestep segments, LinCa adapts to heterogeneous feature dynamics. Experiments on FLUX, Qwen-Image, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LinCa, with less than 0.2% additional parameters, significantly outperforms existing methods and maintains near-lossless quality at 5-7x speedup. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/LinCa

</details>


### [80] [Dual Co-Train: Cross-Dataset Ultrasound Tongue Segmentation Under Extreme Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17983)
*Alisher Myrgyyassov,Zhen Song,Bruce Xiao Wang,Yu Sun,Min Ney Wong,Yihao Zhou,Yongping Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无源域适应的超声舌体轮廓分割框架，基于轻量级UltraUNet骨干网络，通过迭代伪标签精炼、轮廓质量控制和分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格合成数据，实现无需源数据的闭环适应。在12个源-目标迁移对和8个数据集上验证，性能超越包括监督方法在内的所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声舌体轮廓分割在跨数据集域偏移场景下面临挑战：标注样本有限、超声探头差异大、采集噪声多，导致模型泛化能力严重下降。现有方法依赖源域数据或大量标注，无法满足实际临床场景中隐私保护和标注成本限制。

Method: 1) 以仅5张标注源图像预训练UltraUNet模拟欠拟合受限源模型；2) 迭代精炼伪标签并利用基于轮廓的质量控制模块过滤不可靠掩码；3) 通过分割引导的条件GAN生成目标风格合成图像-掩码对；4) 学生模型在干净伪标签目标图像、带一致性正则的噪声伪标签及合成样本混合集上训练，实现无源数据闭环适应。

Result: 在8个超声舌体成像数据集的12个源-目标迁移对上评估，完成源大小扩展实验和消融研究。所有对比中，所提框架在分割重叠度和轮廓精度上均优于基线方法（包括有监督方法）。

Conclusion: 任务相关的伪标签精炼与合成目标风格增强能显著提升超声舌体成像的无源域适应性能，验证了无需源数据即可实现鲁棒跨域分割的可行性。

Abstract: Ultrasound tongue contour segmentation remains challenging under cross-dataset domain shift, where limited annotations, probe variability, and acquisition noise often degrade model generalization. We present a source-free domain adaptation framework for robust ultrasound tongue segmentation built on a lightweight UltraUNet backbone. Starting from a checkpoint pretrained on only five labeled source images, simulating an underfitted constrained source model, the proposed method adapts to a fully-unlabeled target domain by iteratively refining pseudo-labels, filtering unreliable masks with a contour-based quality-control module, and generating target-style synthetic image-mask pairs through a segmentation-guided conditional GAN. The student model is then trained on a mixture of clean pseudo-labeled target images, noisy pseudo-labels with consistency regularization, and synthetic samples, enabling closed-loop adaptation without access to source data. We evaluate the method on 12 source-target transfer pairs across eight ultrasound tongue imaging datasets, and conduct source-size scaling experiments and ablation studies. Across all comparisons, the proposed framework improves segmentation overlap and contour accuracy over the baselines, including supervised ones. These results suggest that task-specific pseudo-label refinement and synthetic target-style augmentation can substantially improve source-free adaptation for ultrasound tongue imaging.

</details>


### [81] [GS-Voxel: Fitting-Free Structured Latents for Large-Scale 3DGS Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17988)
*Ming Qian,Zijian Wang,Minchao Sun,Jincheng Xiong,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Chi Wang,Baoquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-Voxel 是一种无需拟合（fitting-free）的结构化潜在表示框架，用于大规模航空 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）场景生成。它将预优化的 3DGS 重建确定性转换为稀疏活性体素，并通过因子化 VAE 分别编码体素几何和局部高斯属性到稀疏 3D 潜在空间中，该潜在空间的大小随占用体素数增长而非受固定图元数量限制。同时，作者训练图像条件下的流模型，并通过重叠感知的平铺推理实现大面积场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展的潜在 3D 生成器通常依赖结构化张量，而预优化的 3DGS 重建本质上是无序、空间不规则且图元数量变化大的，难以直接适配现有生成框架。因此需要一个桥梁，将无序的 3DGS 重建转化为可用的结构化表示，同时避免额外的逐场景优化。

Method: 1) 确定性转换：将兼容的预优化 3DGS 重建转换为稀疏活性体素，保留亚体素位置和渲染属性，无需逐场景优化；2) 因子化 VAE：分别编码体素几何和局部高斯属性，生成稀疏 3D 潜在表示，其容量随占用体素数增长；3) 流模型：在 GS-Voxel 潜在空间中训练图像条件流模型生成航空 3DGS 场景；4) 重叠感知平铺推理：基于卫星视图图像，实现超出单一训练裁剪范围的大面积场景生成。

Result: 实验结果表明 GS-Voxel 能够为预优化的航空 3DGS 重建提供结构化潜在表示，且潜在容量随占用体素数量增长，支持大规模场景生成。

Conclusion: GS-Voxel 成功弥合了无序 3DGS 重建与结构化潜在生成器之间的鸿沟，实现了无需额外优化的可扩展大规模航空 3D 场景生成，其按需增长的潜在容量设计为大规模场景建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Many scalable latent 3D generators operate on structured tensors, whereas pre-optimized 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions are unordered, spatially irregular, and vary widely in primitive count. We present GS-Voxel, a fitting-free structured latent framework, and evaluate it for large-scale aerial 3D Gaussian scene generation. GS-Voxel deterministically converts a compatible pre-optimized 3DGS reconstruction into sparse active voxels without additional per-scene optimization, retaining the sub-voxel positions and rendering attributes of the selected primitives. A GS-specific factorized VAE then separately encodes voxel geometry and local Gaussian attributes into sparse 3D latents whose size grows with the number of occupied voxels rather than being limited by a fixed scene-wide primitive count. We train image-conditioned flow models in the GS-Voxel latent space to generate aerial 3DGS scenes. A key application enabled by GS-Voxel is large-area scene generation: overlap-aware tiled inference extends synthesis beyond a single training crop conditioned on satellite-view images. Our results show that GS-Voxel provides structured latents for pre-optimized aerial 3DGS reconstructions, with latent capacity that grows with the number of occupied voxels.

</details>


### [82] [AViTS: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Token Selection for Efficient Dynamic-Resolution Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17995)
*Haoran Qin,Zhengan Yan,Shikang Zheng,Xiaobing Tu,Jiacheng Liu,Yuqi Lin,Chang Zou,JinShan Liu,Peiliang Cai,Xiantao Zhang,Jinkui Ren,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AViTS框架，一种自适应时空token选择机制，用于动态分辨率DiT，通过融合潜在文本注意力和跨时间步token特征变化，实现重要性感知的选择性上采样，在保持质量的同时大幅减少计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态分辨率采样虽然降低了早期去噪成本，但均匀上采样所有潜在token会造成冗余计算，并可能损害细节一致性。现有部分上采样策略依赖局部潜在结构线索或单步统计，难以同时捕捉token-文本语义相关性和跨扩散步的token表示动态。

Method: AViTS通过三个核心组件实现：1)空间重要性建模，利用潜在文本注意力捕捉token与文本的语义相关性；2)时间重要性建模，通过跨扩散时间步的token级特征变化衡量动态显著性；3)融合两种重要性进行时空感知的选择性上采样，优先对关键token进行分辨率细化，推迟不重要的token。

Result: 在FLUX上实现高达6.34x的加速，在Qwen-Image-Edit和FLUX.1-Kontext-dev上实现近9倍FLOPs缩减；与知识蒸馏、量化和特征缓存正交，结合蒸馏模型可达14.76x加速。

Conclusion: AViTS通过时空联合的token重要性评估，有效解决了动态分辨率DiT中均匀上采样的冗余计算问题，显著改善了质量-效率权衡，且与其他优化技术正交可叠加使用。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve high-quality generation but are costly due to iterative sampling. Dynamic-resolution sampling reduces early-stage cost by denoising at low resolution; however, uniformly upsampling all latent tokens at resolution transitions incurs redundant computation and may degrade fine-detail consistency. Existing partial upsampling strategies typically rely on local latent structure cues or single-step statistics, making it difficult to jointly capture token-text semantic relevance and token-wise representation dynamics across diffusion steps. We propose AViTS, an adaptive spatiotemporal token selection framework for dynamic-resolution DiTs. AViTS models spatial importance via latent-text attention and temporal importance via token-level feature variation across diffusion timesteps, and fuses them to enable spatiotemporal importance-aware selective upsampling: it prioritizes resolution refinement for critical tokens while deferring less important ones, thereby reducing redundant high-resolution computation and improving the quality-efficiency trade-off. AViTS achieves up to 6.34x on FLUX and nearly 9x FLOPs reduction on Qwen-Image-Edit and FLUX.1-Kontext-dev, orthogonal to distillation, quantization, and feature caching, and reaching 14.76x with distilled models. Code: https://github.com/QHR69/AViTS

</details>


### [83] [Memory Tree Guided Key Frame Querying for Efficient 3D Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18009)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Fu-Chen Chen,Li-Wu Tsao,Cheng-Han Lee,Che-Chun Su,Lu Xia,Ronghui Peng,Jenq-Neng Hwang,Min Sun,Cheng-Hao Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MemTree3D，一种基于记忆树的3D场景表示方法，用于在具身场景中高效进行3D问答。通过构建紧凑且可复用的3D场景表示，支持实时在线构建，使大语言模型能够高效查询并检索与问题相关的关键帧，无需重新处理整个视频流。在OpenEQA数据集上，该方法在LLM-Match指标上分别提升GPT-4o 17.4%、LLaVA-OneVision-7B 5.8%，优于现有视觉搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉搜索关键帧检索方法需要针对每个用户查询在数千个视频帧中进行视觉搜索，效率低下且计算资源开销大。在具身场景中，由于计算和内存资源有限，需要更高效的VLM推理方法。

Method: 提出记忆树引导的关键帧选择范式（MemTree3D）：1）构建紧凑且可复用的3D场景表示，利用相机6-DoF位姿支持实时在线构建；2）该表示捕获多层级3D场景信息；3）通过基于评分的帧选择机制，使LLM能够高效查询和检索与问题相关的关键帧，无需重新处理整个视频流。

Result: 在OpenEQA基准上，LLM-Match指标中GPT-4o提升17.4%，LLaVA-OneVision-7B提升5.8%，性能优于现有的视觉搜索方法。

Conclusion: MemTree3D通过记忆树引导的关键帧选择范式，有效解决了具身场景中3D问答的计算与内存资源限制问题，实现了更高效、更准确的VLM推理。

Abstract: Answering questions accurately and efficiently in embodied scenarios presents significant challenges due to limited computational and memory resources for Vision Language Model (VLM) inference. Existing methods adopt visual search key frame retrieval method to select critical question-related key frames for VLM input. However, visual search methods are inefficient because they require visual search among thousands of video frames for each individual user query. In this work, we propose a memory tree guided key frame selection paradigm for efficient 3D question answering in embodied scenarios. Our method leverages a compact and reusable 3D scene representation, termed MemTree3D, which supports real-time online construction leveraging camera 6-DoF poses. MemTree3D captures multi-level 3D scene information, enabling a Large Language Model to efficiently query and retrieve question-relevant key frames through our scoring-based frame selection without reprocessing the entire video stream. On OpenEQA, our method improves the LLM-Match of GPT-4o by 17.4%, LLaVA-OneVision-7B by 5.8%, outperforms existing visual search methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/MemTree3D

</details>


### [84] [Automated ACL Footprint Identification Using 3D Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18012)
*Ruida Cheng,Ali Uneri,Gabriel Gibson,Frances T. Sheehan,Barry Boden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索了两种3D深度学习架构用于直接从3D磁共振图像中识别前交叉韧带（ACL）股骨足迹中心。图像基础的方法（平均误差2.1mm）优于网格基础的方法（平均误差2.8mm），为ACL重建术中精确隧道定位提供了可行的临床AI辅助方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACL重建失败的常见原因是股骨隧道位置不当，准确的ACL股骨足迹识别对手术精度至关重要。现有AI研究主要集中于ACL分割和撕裂分类，尚未深入研究足迹中心识别，因此需要探索深度学习在该领域的应用。

Method: 开发了两种3D深度学习架构：（1）基于3D图卷积神经网络的几何模型，应用于3D股骨网格；（2）基于3D MR图像的标志点增强识别模型。使用公开数据库中4883个右膝和3087个左膝图像集，80%用于训练，20%用于测试。

Result: 两种模型均表现出优异性能，其中基于图像的方法优于基于模型的方法，平均误差分别为2.1mm和2.8mm。

Conclusion: 3D深度学习为ACL足迹定位提供了可行的临床方法，有潜力提高ACL重建手术中足迹定位的准确性，从而改善术后膝关节健康并降低移植失败风险。

Abstract: One of the most common reasons for anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) reconstruction failure is femoral tunnel malpositioning (ACL footprint center and tunnel orientation). Such failures may lead to the development of meniscal pathology and osteoarthritis. Accurate ACL femoral footprint identification is therefore essential for precise tunnel placement, restoration of the native knee joint mechanics, post-surgical knee joint health and prevention of graft failure. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) bring new opportunities to improve image-guided orthopedic surgery. However, at present, existing AI research focuses primarily on ACL segmentation and rupture classification based on pre- and post-operative magnetic resonance (MR) images. Identification of the ACL footprint center using deep learning methods has not been thoroughly researched. Thus, the purpose of this study is to explore 3D deep learning models for ACL femoral footprint identification directly from 3D MR images. Two comprehensive 3D deep learning architectures were developed: a 3D graph convolutional neural network-based geometric model applied to 3D femoral meshes; and a 3D landmark-enhanced identification model based on 3D MR images. A total of 4883 right and 3087 left knee image sets were used from a publicly available database. Eighty percent (80%) were applied to model generation, and twenty percent (20%) were preserved for model testing. Both models achieved excellent performance; however, the image-based method outperformed the model-based method (average error of 2.1mm vs 2.8 mm). Thus, 3D deep learning provides a feasible clinical approach for ACL footprint localization and has the potential to improve ACL reconstruction footprint accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [Initialization-Free Bundle Adjustment Revisited: A Controlled Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18028)
*Simon Weber,Mateo de Mayo,Je Hyeong Hong,Carl Olsson,Daniel Cremers,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的评估框架（C++实现OSE公式 + Blender数据集生成器）对初始化自由的光束法平差（InitFree BA）进行实验研究，发现了一个此前被忽视的"优化-重建差距"问题：低OSE目标值的射影解在度量升级后可能导致显著不同的欧氏重建结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有InitFree BA研究主要评估优化是否成功，但未明确回答"低OSE目标值是否必然产生有效的度量3D重建"这一关键问题，需要系统的实验验证。

Method: 构建统一评估框架：1) 用C++实现现有OSE公式；2) 用Blender生成具有精确真值和可控相机配置/观测密度的数据集；3) 对比优化误差与最终重建质量，分析初始化先验、地标观测密度和度量升级稳定性等因素。

Result: 实验揭示了优化-重建差距：具有相似低OSE值的射影解在度量升级后可能产生差异显著的欧氏重建。识别出初始化先验、地标观测密度和度量升级稳定性是控制重建成功的关键因素。

Conclusion: InitFree BA的主要挑战并非单纯最小化OSE目标，而是获得能够可靠进行度量升级的射影重建。本文提出的基准、实现和分析为后续研究建立了更坚实的实验基础。

Abstract: Initialization-free bundle adjustment (InitFree BA) aims to recover camera poses and scene structure directly from image observations, avoiding the geometric initialization stages of conventional structure-from-motion pipelines. Recent methods based on Object-Space Error (OSE) formulations and Variable Projection (VarPro) show encouraging optimization behavior from random camera configurations. However, existing evaluations primarily measure optimization success, leaving unclear whether a low OSE objective yields a valid metric 3D reconstruction. We revisit InitFree BA experimentally through a unified evaluation framework combining a C++ implementation of existing OSE formulations with a Blender-based dataset generator providing exact ground truth and controlled camera configurations and observation densities. Our experiments reveal a previously overlooked optimization--reconstruction gap: projective solutions with similarly low OSE values can lead to substantially different Euclidean reconstructions after metric upgrade. We identify initialization priors, landmark observation density, and metric-upgrade stability as key factors governing reconstruction success. Overall, our results suggest that the main challenge of InitFree BA is not merely minimizing OSE objectives, but obtaining projective reconstructions that admit reliable metric upgrade. We believe that the proposed benchmark, implementation, and analysis establish stronger experimental foundations for future research on initialization-free bundle adjustment, a problem largely unexplored within the computer vision community. Project page is available at https://github.com/simonwebertum/InitFreeBA.git.

</details>


### [86] [Deep Academic Survey: Stateful Agentic Closed-Loop Paradigm for Academic Survey Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18034)
*Zhikai Xu,Zhucun Xue,Teng Hu,Yabiao Wang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAS，一个有状态的智能体框架，用于生成面向出版级别的学术综述。其核心思路是将可复用的论文分析（基于约200万篇论文的DAS-2M元数据湖）与特定主题的手稿构建相分离，通过显式的文献、组织、写作和定稿状态，以及候选引导的分类规划、反向论文到章节路由、层级声明与引用规划，实现从论文理解到稿件验证的闭环。同时引入DAS-Bench（30主题基准）和DAS-Eval（16条标准评估），实验表明DAS在四个维度上均取得最高平均分（总分4.34 vs 最强对手4.03），盲审专家也更偏好DAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Deep Research和自动综述生成系统只覆盖综述构建的部分环节，未能通过一个共享、可修改的状态来协调论文理解、文献组织、证据支撑的写作和稿件验证四步流程，导致综述构建缺乏连贯性和可修正性。

Method: DAS将可复用的论文分析与特定主题的稿件构建解耦：基于DAS-2M（约200万篇论文的调查导向表示）元数据湖，智能体通过显式的文献、组织、写作和定稿四类状态推进工作；采用候选引导的分类规划、反向论文到章节路由、层级声明与引用规划等方法；语义审查只对受影响的写作状态进行定向修复和重新评估，形成限定范围的闭环并配以确定性验证。

Result: 在全部30个主题上，DAS在学术引用质量、分类综合、层级语篇和稿件组装可靠性四个维度均取得最高平均分（总分4.34 vs 最强竞争对手4.03），且在匹配的21个CS子集上排序一致。盲审专家评估中，DAS在30个主题中的27个优于Naive RAG，在21个共享CS主题中的19个优于AutoSurvey。

Conclusion: DAS通过有状态、可修正的智能体框架，成功将论文分析、文献组织、证据支撑写作和稿件验证整合为闭环，显著提升了学术综述的生成质量，为自动化高质量学术综述生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Academic surveys play a central role in organizing rapidly expanding scholarly literature, yet their construction requires extensive paper analysis, coherent knowledge organization, fine-grained citation support, and reliable manuscript assembly. Existing Deep Research and automated survey generation systems address parts of this process, but typically do not coordinate paper understanding, literature organization, evidence-grounded drafting, and manuscript validation through a shared, revisable state. We introduce DAS, a stateful agentic framework for generating publication-oriented academic surveys. Its key idea is to separate reusable paper analysis from topic-specific manuscript construction. DAS builds on DAS-2M, a dynamically updated metadata lake containing survey-oriented representations of approximately two million papers. Its agents maintain explicit literature, organization, writing, and finalization states through candidate-grounded taxonomy planning, reverse paper-to-section routing, and hierarchical claim and citation planning. Semantic review reactivates only the affected writing states for repair and reevaluation, forming a scoped closed loop with deterministic validation. We further introduce DAS-Bench, a 30-topic benchmark, together with DAS-Eval, which assesses scholarly citation quality, taxonomic synthesis, hierarchical discourse, and manuscript assembly reliability through 16 criteria. Among systems evaluated on all 30 topics, DAS achieves the highest average in all four dimensions, with an overall score of 4.34 compared with 4.03 for the strongest competitor, and the same ordering is preserved on the matched 21-topic CS subset. Blinded expert evaluation further prefers DAS to Naive RAG on 27 of 30 topics and to AutoSurvey on 19 of 21 shared CS topics. The project page is available at https://zhikaixu24.github.io/projects/DAS/.

</details>


### [87] [Plug-and-Play Traffic Element Awareness for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18035)
*Zongzheng Zhang,Jijun Wang,Saining Zhang,Shuo Wang,Yiru Wang,Hai Yang,Yang Chen,Yuwen Heng,Hao Sun,Anqing Jiang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了交通要素（如交通灯、路标）感知对端到端自动驾驶的影响。作者通过为多个公开数据集补充全面的交通要素标注，构建了统一的研究基础设施，并采用即插即用的最小化集成设计将交通要素信号引入现有驾驶管线。实验覆盖多种现代范式（感知-预测-规划、VLA、回归规划器、扩散策略、轨迹评分框架）和多个数据集，结果表明这种简单集成在所有范式和数据集上均一致提升了驾驶性能，并在NAVSIM-v2基准上取得了新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶研究主要关注动态道路参与者（如车辆和行人），而交通要素（交通灯、路标）的作用一直未被充分探索。公共数据集缺乏结构化的交通要素标注，且现代驾驶系统架构和训练范式差异巨大，导致社区缺乏量化交通要素影响的系统性研究。

Method: 1) 构建统一研究基础设施：为多个公开驾驶数据集增强全面的交通要素标注；2) 采用最小化且通用的集成设计，以即插即用方式将交通要素信号融入现有管线，几乎无需架构修改；3) 在多种现代范式（感知-预测-规划管线、VLA模型、回归规划器、扩散策略、轨迹评分框架）和多个数据集（nuScenes、NAVSIM-v1/v2、Bench2Drive）上进行系统评估。

Result: 在所有范式和数据集上，简单的交通要素集成一致地提升了驾驶性能，证明交通要素感知是端到端驾驶系统中稳健且可泛化的信号。在具有挑战性的NAVSIM-v2基准上，该方法显著改进了SOTA架构和数据管线，确立了新的最优表现。

Conclusion: 交通要素感知为各类端到端驾驶系统提供了稳健且可泛化的性能增益，值得成为端到端自动驾驶研究的关键组成部分，该工作填补了该领域系统研究的空白。

Abstract: Traffic elements such as traffic lights and road signs play a fundamental role in human driving decisions and should naturally influence end-to-end driving performance. However, existing end-to-end driving research predominantly focuses on dynamic road participants (e.g., vehicles and pedestrians), while the role of traffic elements remains largely unexplored. The community still lacks a systematic study quantifying their impact, largely because public datasets rarely provide structured traffic-element annotations and modern driving systems vary widely in architecture and training paradigm. In this work, we present the first systematic investigation of traffic element awareness for end-to-end autonomous driving. We construct a unified research infrastructure by augmenting multiple public driving datasets with comprehensive traffic-element annotations. To support diverse model families, we adopt a minimal and universal integration design that incorporates traffic-element signals into existing pipelines in a plug-and-play manner with negligible architectural modification. We evaluate this design across modern paradigms, including perception-prediction-planning pipelines, vision-language-action models (VLA), regression-based planners, diffusion-based policies, and trajectory-scoring frameworks, on nuScenes, NAVSIM-v1, NAVSIM-v2, and Bench2Drive. Across all paradigms and datasets, this simple integration consistently improves driving performance, demonstrating that traffic element awareness provides a robust and generalizable signal for end-to-end driving systems. Notably, on the challenging NAVSIM-v2 benchmark, our approach significantly improves state-of-the-art architectures and data pipelines, establishing a new state of the art.

</details>


### [88] [EDITBRIDGE: Towards Faithful and Efficient Ultra-High-Resolution Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18063)
*Jiayi Song,Shijie Huang,Fangtai Wu,Yubo Huang,Zhenxiong Tan,Songhua Liu,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditBridge提出了一种扩散桥框架，用于高效超高分辨率图像编辑，通过将细化建模为从低分辨率编辑结果到高分辨率对应物的结构化数据到数据翻译，并引入先验引导的块状稀疏注意力机制，实现了高达4K分辨率的高保真编辑，同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型受限于二次注意力复杂度和内存需求，只能处理1K以下分辨率。常见的两阶段方案（低分辨率编辑+独立超分辨率）存在信息偏差（幻觉细节与原始HR源矛盾）和纹理退化（过度平滑或过度锐化伪影）两大问题。

Method: 1) 将细化过程建模为从LR编辑结果到HR对应物的数据到数据翻译（扩散桥框架），而非从噪声重新生成；2) 显式以原始HR源为条件以保留真实细节；3) 引入先验引导的块状稀疏注意力机制，利用第一阶段编辑的语义对应关系，将跨图像交互限制在空间对齐区域，显著降低计算开销。

Result: EditBridge在高达4K分辨率下实现高保真编辑和优越的感知质量，在2K分辨率下提供3.6-8.4倍的加速，并支持61秒内完成实际的4K编辑。

Conclusion: EditBridge有效解决了超高分辨率编辑中的信息偏差和纹理退化问题，通过扩散桥框架和稀疏注意力机制在保持编辑质量的同时实现了显著的计算效率提升，为实际4K工作流铺平了道路。

Abstract: High-resolution image editing is increasingly demanded in professional workflows, yet existing diffusion-based models remain constrained to resolutions below 1K due to quadratic attention complexity and prohibitive memory requirements. A prevalent workaround employs a two-stage pipeline: editing at low resolution followed by independent super-resolution. However, this approach suffers from two critical issues: information divergence, where hallucinated details contradict the original high-resolution (HR) source, and texture degradation, manifesting as over-smoothed or over-sharpened artifacts. We propose EditBridge, a diffusion bridge framework for efficient ultra high-resolution editing. Unlike conventional diffusion that regenerates from noise, we formulate refinement as structured data-to-data translation from the low-resolution (LR) edited result to its HR counterpart, explicitly conditioned on the original HR source to preserve authentic details. To efficiently incorporate HR source guidance, we introduce a prior-guided block-wise sparse attention mechanism that exploits semantic correspondence from first-stage editing to constrain cross-image interactions to spatially aligned regions, significantly reducing computational overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EditBridge achieves high-fidelity editing with superior perceptual quality at resolutions up to 4K, delivering 3.6--8.4$\times$ speedup at 2K and enabling practical 4K editing in 61 seconds.

</details>


### [89] [From Corpora to Co-Evolving Capabilities: Capability-Centric Data Design for Generalist Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18076)
*Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Taihang Hu,Jun Zheng,Qing Jin,Qinye Zhou,Zhengtao Wu,Yongchao Du,Zuan Gao,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Yuhang Yu,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能力驱动的数据基础设施，通过三个协同的数据引擎（文本-图像对齐、图像间变换、图像-知识关联）和与能力获取依赖顺序对齐的多阶段课程学习，构建了440M图像T2I语料库、120M编辑对和27M+图像实体对，并基于此从零训练了3B和6B两种规模的多模态扩散模型，在CPI-Bench等基准上展示了广泛的视觉覆盖和跨能力迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像生成流水线孤立地优化各自任务的数据集，缺乏对异构监督数据的统一组织方式。核心挑战不仅在于如何策划每个任务专属语料，更在于如何根据生成能力之间的依赖关系来组织异构监督信号。

Method: 提出能力驱动数据基础设施：(1) 三个专门化但可互操作的数据引擎分别构建文本-图像对齐、图像间变换、图像-知识关联的互补关系监督；(2) 字幕专家对齐T2I和编辑监督；(3) 多阶段课程学习沿能力获取的依赖顺序逐步演化任务组成、视觉概念分布、数据质量和图像分辨率；(4) 能力感知评估通过目标检索、专家构建和差距感知重采样闭环反馈。最终构建440M T2I语料、120M编辑对和27M+图像实体对，训练3B和6B两种规模的扩散模型。

Result: 在CPI-Bench上进行了定量评估，并在多种T2I和编辑场景中进行定性评估。实验结果表明该框架实现了广泛的视觉覆盖、多样化的渲染能力以及跨生成能力的有效迁移。

Conclusion: 能力驱动的数据基础设施通过将数据策划与能力依赖关系对齐，能够有效统一异构监督信号，显著提升多模态扩散模型在多种生成任务上的表现与迁移能力。

Abstract: Large-scale image generation has benefited from advances in data scale, quality, rebalancing, and recaptioning, yet conventional pipelines typically optimize task-specific datasets in isolation. A central challenge is not only how to curate each task-specific corpus, but also how to organize heterogeneous supervision according to the dependencies among generative capabilities. We present a \textbf{capability-driven data infrastructure} that couples capability-specific supervision construction with capability-aligned curriculum scheduling. Its three specialized yet interoperable data engines build complementary relational supervision for text-image grounding, inter-image transformation, and image-knowledge association, while caption experts align T2I and editing supervision across tasks and granularities. A multi-stage curriculum jointly evolves task composition, visual-concept distribution, data quality, and image resolution along the dependency order of capability acquisition, with capability-aware evaluation closing the loop through targeted retrieval, expert construction, and gap-aware resampling. At scale, the framework curates a 440M-image T2I corpus, 120M editing pairs, and over 27M image-entity pairs. With this infrastructure, we train multimodal diffusion models at two scales from scratch, with 3B and 6B sizes respectively. We conduct quantitative evaluation on CPI-Bench, along with qualitative evaluations across diverse text-to-image and editing scenarios. Experimental results present broad visual coverage, versatile rendering, and effective transfer across generative capabilities.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Margin-Regularized Structured Semantic Alignment for Brain-Language Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16975)
*Jiaqi Wang,Huawen Hu,Shu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MD-SigLIP，一种基于边际正则化的结构化语义对齐框架，用于脑-语言解码。该框架直接将大脑嵌入与文本嵌入对齐到共享语义空间，通过检索实现解码，并引入listwise边际正则化项强制正语义簇与负样本之间的结构化排序约束，从而明确建模神经表征与语言语义之间的对应关系，在多个评估设置下达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的脑-语言解码方法虽然取得显著进展，但解码内容是否真正反映神经表征、还是很大程度上由语言模型自身重建，这一问题尚不明确。这种模糊性限制了方法的可解释性，也阻碍了对大脑与语言内在对应关系的研究。

Method: 提出MD-SigLIP框架：1) 直接在共享语义空间中对齐大脑嵌入与文本嵌入，实现基于检索的解码；2) 在重复感知的sigmoid对比学习基础上，引入listwise边际正则化项，强制正语义簇与负样本之间的结构化排序约束；3) 同时建模多正例语义结构和基于边际的排序，以捕捉神经信号中反映的语言嵌入流形组织。

Result: 在完整词汇表和子集两种评估设置下，该方法的检索性能均达到最先进水平（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模神经表征与语言语义之间的结构化对应关系，MD-SigLIP不仅提升了解码性能，更增强了解码结果的可解释性，为研究大脑与语言的内在联系提供了更可靠的途径。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models, brain-language decoding has achieved remarkable progress. However, it remains unclear whether decoded content genuinely reflects neural representations or is largely reconstructed by the language model itself. This ambiguity limits interpretability and hinders the investigation of intrinsic brain-language correspondence. To address this challenge, we propose MD-SigLIP. This margin-regularized structured semantic alignment framework directly aligns brain embeddings with text embeddings in a shared semantic space, enabling retrieval-based decoding. This formulation enables explicit modeling of the correspondence between neural representations and language semantics. Building upon duplicate-aware sigmoid contrastive learning, we introduce a listwise margin-regularized term that enforces structured ranking constraints between positive semantic clusters and negative samples. By modeling multi-positive semantic structure and margin-based ordering simultaneously, the method captures the manifold organization of language embeddings reflected in neural signals. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art retrieval performance under both full-vocabulary and subset evaluation settings.

</details>


### [91] [Cross-Model Memory Transfer via Target-Side Reader Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17050)
*Mingyuan Li,Guangsheng Yu,Xu Wang,Shaoxiong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Engram式哈希记忆在不同骨干模型间迁移时的关键因素，发现冻结的记忆表格本身和正确的寻址都很重要，但迁移表格只有在与目标模型对齐的阅读器配合下才有用。通过双层四分支阅读器，跨模型复用几乎可以弥合同模型与跨模型之间的差距（平均得分38.8），且当阅读器接口兼容时，冻结记忆可直接提供实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的知识使用改进方法分为非参数检索和参数化适配两个阵营，各有局限。Engram式哈希记忆处于中间地带，但将其迁移到不同骨干模型时，究竟是冻结的记忆内容更重要还是目标端的阅读器更重要，这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 通过跨模型冻结记忆提取实验：在源模型上训练记忆，冻结后附加到不同目标模型，仅训练轻量级阅读器。进行消融实验分析记忆内容和寻址的影响，并设计双层四分支阅读器架构，同时考察阅读器直接兼容与适配两种场景。

Result: 消融实验表明学习到的记忆内容和正确寻址都重要，但迁移表格需通过与目标模型对齐的阅读器才能发挥作用。双层四分支阅读器在受控评估下达到38.8平均分，几乎弥合同模型与跨模型的差距。阅读器接口直接兼容时，冻结记忆无需训练即可提供实用价值，可选适配还能进一步提升。

Conclusion: Engram可以作为一种可复用的外部知识工件，前提是目标模型能访问兼容的阅读器接口；当直接复用阅读器不足时，目标端适配可进一步改善对齐效果。

Abstract: Methods for improving knowledge use in large language models typically fall into two regimes. Non-parametric retrieval offers flexible access to external knowledge, but adds retrieval latency, context overhead, and only shallow integration with the backbone. Parametric adaptation is efficient at inference time, but entangles knowledge with model weights and can be hard to update, audit, or transfer. Engram-style hashed memory occupies a middle regime: it stores learned information in an external, addressable table, yet consumes that table through a small learned reader. This raises a basic question: when such a memory is moved across backbones, what matters more, the frozen memory itself or the target-side reader? We study this question through cross-model frozen-memory extraction, in which a memory trained on a source model is frozen and attached to a different target model, with only a lightweight reader trained. Ablations show that learned memory content and correct addressing both matter, but the transferred table becomes useful only through a reader aligned to the target model. In downstream question answering tasks, a dual-layer, four-branch reader nearly closes the gap between same-model and cross-model reuse, achieving an average score of 38.8 under our controlled evaluation protocol. Moreover, when the provider reader is directly compatible with the target interface, the frozen artifact can provide substantial utility without target-side training, while optional reader adaptation yields further improvement. These results suggest that Engram can serve as a reusable external knowledge artifact, provided that the target has access to a compatible reader interface; target-side adaptation can further improve alignment when direct reader reuse is insufficient.

</details>


### [92] [Institution-Specific LLM Prompting Recovers PHI That De-identification Systems and Their Gold Standards Both Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17051)
*Daniel Palacios,Matthew Brady Neeley,Angel Adetomike Otto,Shalini Dhamodharan,John P. Woodhouse,Chi-fan Lin,Mark Zobeck,Zhandong Liu,Hyun-Hwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在得克萨斯儿童医院的100份儿科肿瘤病历上，评估了8种大语言模型（LLM）结合上下文学习（ICL）进行去识别化的表现。通过三种渐进的提示策略，发现LLM不仅超越了专用系统（F1=0.918 vs TiDE的0.779），还解决了机构特有PHI识别问题，并通过"按需校准"实现了召回率0.981。研究建议将机构特定提示开发作为主要的适应策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录的二次利用需要去识别化，但现有系统会遗漏机构特定的受保护健康信息（PHI），如医院缩写、建筑物名称和内部代码，这些信息的PHI状态由本地决定。作者希望探究LLM结合上下文学习能否弥合这一差距，并控制精确率-召回率的权衡。

Method: 在100份标注的儿科肿瘤笔记（5,322个PHI片段）上，对比8种LLM和两种专用系统（Stanford TiDE、OpenMed PII）及两种基于模式的基线。每种LLM在三种特异性递增的提示下运行：(1) HIPAA对齐基线；(2) 基线加上遗漏的机构PHI类别；(3) 提示2加上避免过度删除临床内容的指令。随后比较14种多智能体和集成配置与最佳单提示的性能。

Result: LLM显著优于专用系统（最佳F1=0.918±0.001 vs TiDE 0.779），优势集中在上下文相关的PHI类别。命名遗漏类别恢复了79%（48/61）的遗漏，劝阻过度删除恢复了精确率。最优单提示（F1 0.906-0.907）优于所有智能体架构。LLM输出揭示了414个候选标注缺口，重新标注确认了227个PHI片段，最终提示达到召回率0.981（F1=0.907±0.002）。

Conclusion: 校准良好的上下文学习可以通过单次LLM调用同时解决机构PHI缺口和精确率-召回率权衡。LLM运行成本高于传统方法，但成本换来的是审计参考标准的能力。LLM是专用去识别化系统的合法且可适应的替代方案，机构特定的提示开发应作为主要适应策略。

Abstract: Secondary use of electronic health records requires de-identification, yet existing systems miss \emph{institutionally situated} protected health information (PHI) such as hospital abbreviations, building names, and internal codes whose status is locally determined. We ask whether large language models (LLMs) with in-context learning (ICL) can close this gap and control the precision--recall trade-off.
  On 100 annotated pediatric oncology notes (5,322 PHI spans) from Texas Children's Hospital, we benchmarked eight LLMs against two purpose-built systems (Stanford TiDE, OpenMed PII) and two pattern-based baselines. Each LLM ran under three prompts of increasing specificity: (1) a HIPAA-aligned baseline, (2) baseline plus the institutional PHI categories it missed, and (3) prompt 2 plus instructions against over-redacting clinical content. We then compared 14~multi-agent and ensemble configurations against the best single prompt, with recall the primary safety metric.
  LLMs outperformed the purpose-built systems (best F1=0.918$\pm$0.001 vs.\ TiDE 0.779), with advantages concentrated in contextual categories. Naming the missed categories recovered 79\% (48/61) of them, and discouraging over-redaction restored precision. No agentic architecture beat calibrated single-pass prompting (F1 0.906--0.907), but LLM outputs surfaced 414~candidate annotation gaps; re-annotation confirmed 227~PHI spans, against which the final prompt reached recall=0.981 (F1=0.907$\pm$0.002).
  Well-calibrated ICL resolves both the institutional PHI gap and the precision--recall trade-off in one LLM call per note. LLMs cost more to run than traditional methods, but that cost buys a way to audit the reference standard.
  LLMs are a legitimate, adaptable alternative to purpose-built de-identification systems; institution-specific prompt development should be the primary adaptation strategy.

</details>


### [93] [SpeechSense: A Paralinguistic-Focused Dataset for Fine-Grained Speech Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17931)
*Shicheng Ma,Wenqian Cui,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpeechSense，一个用于细粒度语音情感分析的新数据集，专注于通过韵律线索而非词汇内容来检测人际立场（stance），包含8类专门分类体系，并通过语音合成和人工验证构建。实验证明具备声学信息的模型优于纯文本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感分析研究存在两大局限：一是主流方法依赖文本中心流水线（ASR+文本分析），会丢失韵律、语调等关键声学特征，无法捕捉声学歧义语句中的态度含义；二是现有基准的标签粒度不匹配，偏向基础情绪（如快乐、悲伤）而非人际立场（如自信、不耐烦），缺乏社会敏感性所需的多层次情感理解。

Method: 提出 SpeechSense 数据集：1）定义8类人际立场分类体系，这些立场主要通过韵律线索而非词汇内容来检测；2）基于该分类体系构建精选数据集，通过高保真语音合成和严格人工验证完成；3）在多模态LLM、纯文本LLM和语音编码器上进行综合实验对比。

Result: 实验表明，具备声学访问能力的模型始终优于纯文本基线模型，实证验证了声学线索在检测细微说话者态度中的首要地位，凸显了SpeechSense数据集的必要性。

Conclusion: SpeechSense填补了语音情感分析中细粒度人际立场感知的空白，验证了声学线索优于文本线索的核心假设，为未来语音情感分析研究提供了更合适的基准和数据资源。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have revolutionized speech processing, yet effective speech understanding requires discerning not just what is said, but how it is said. Speech Sentiment Analysis plays a critical role in decoding these paralinguistic cues for diverse real-world applications such as recruitment and customer service. However, existing Speech Sentiment Analysis research faces two primary limitations. First, dominant approaches rely on text-centric pipelines that cascade Automatic Speech Recognition with text analysis. This process inevitably discards essential acoustic features like prosody and tone, failing to capture attitudinal meanings in acoustically ambiguous utterances. Second, current benchmarks suffer from a mismatch in label granularity, prioritizing basic emotions (e.g., happy, sad) over the nuanced interpersonal stances (e.g., confident, impatient) necessary for social sensitivity. To address these limitations, we propose a novel dataset, SpeechSense, for fine-grained speech sentiment analysis. Specifically, we define a specialized 8-class taxonomy of interpersonal stances detectable primarily through prosodic cues beyond lexical content alone. We then construct a curated dataset based on this taxonomy, built from high-fidelity speech synthesis and rigorous human validation. Comprehensive experiments across multi-modal LLMs, text-only LLMs, and speech encoders demonstrate that models with acoustic access consistently outperform text-only baselines. These results empirically validate the primacy of acoustic cues in detecting subtle speaker attitudes, highlighting the necessity of SpeechSense. Dataset and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Sher13cked/SpeechSense.

</details>


### [94] [Foundation Agents Meet Agentic Deep Research: Evidence-Grounded Clinical Code Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17075)
*Junda Wang,Meysam Ghaffari,Akshat Choube,Mohsen Sharifi Renani,Hong Yu,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ICD-Deepresearch，一种将结构化EHR基础模型与语言基础模型结合的DeepResearch工作流，用于下一次就诊的ICD编码预测。该方法通过候选生成（SparseEHR生成EHR先验+GPT-5直接预测）和最终选择（验证、去重、联合排序）在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上分别达到24.60/35.09%和25.14/48.32%的患者平均精确率/召回率，且医生对检索文档的有用性评分（51%和68%）显著优于GPT-5网页搜索（22%/39%）和Medical Deep Research（32%/41%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一次就诊ICD编码预测是前瞻性多标签任务，目标笔记尚不存在且多个编码可能正确。结构化EHR基础模型擅长捕捉复发和时间进展，而语言基础模型能生成灵活的诊断假设，但两者各有局限，需要组合互补以提升预测和证据检索质量。

Method: 提出ICD-Deepresearch工作流，由三部分构成：(1)候选生成阶段使用SparseEHR产生EHR先验，初始化两轮有界研究扩展，同时独立的GPT-5直接预测提供互补候选；(2)最终选择阶段验证、去重并联合排序两条路径的候选；(3)单独的模块在不改变预测的情况下撰写推理依据。整体流程将患者证据、外部临床关系和精确编码语义在固定top-K预算下连接起来。

Result: 在MIMIC-III上达到24.60/35.09%的患者平均精确率/召回率，在MIMIC-IV上达到25.14/48.32%。医生对检索文档的有用性评分为51%（MIMIC-III）和68%（MIMIC-IV），优于独立GPT-5网页搜索（22%/39%）和Medical Deep Research（32%/41%），且优于已注册的本地对照系统。

Conclusion: ICD-Deepresearch通过组合结构化EHR基础模型和语言基础模型，在ICD编码预测性能上优于本地对照，同时检索的医学证据获得医生更高的有用性评分，证明了混合预测+研究检索工作流在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Next-encounter ICD forecasting predicts which standardized diagnosis codes will be documented at a future visit from the longitudinal record available beforehand. The task is prospective and multi-label: the target note does not yet exist, and several codes may be correct. Structured EHR foundation models capture recurrence and temporal progression, whereas language foundation models generate flexible diagnostic hypotheses. We introduce ICD-Deepresearch, a DeepResearch workflow that composes these predictive foundation models with medical search and ICD dictionaries. Because no source reveals the future code set, research evaluates candidate transitions by linking patient evidence, external clinical relations, and exact code semantics under a fixed top-K budget. Candidate Generation uses SparseEHR to produce an EHR Prior that initializes two bounded Research Expansion rounds; an independent GPT-5 Direct Forecast supplies complementary candidates. Final Selection validates, deduplicates, and jointly ranks both paths, after which a separate module writes rationales without changing predictions. Finally ICD-Deepresearch achieves patient-averaged precision/recall of 24.60/35.09% on MIMIC-III and 25.14/48.32% on MIMIC-IV. Physicians rate 51% and 68% of its retrieved documents useful, compared with 22% and 39% for standalone GPT-5 web search and 32% and 41% for Medical Deep Research. ICD-Deepresearch therefore improves over the registered local comparators while retrieving evidence with higher physician-rated usefulness than the standalone research systems

</details>


### [95] [Uncertainty-Aware Decision Making in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17084)
*Abderrahmene Boudiaf,Irfan Hussain,Sajid Javed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）不确定性研究的综述，围绕"决策中心"框架组织文献：不确定性来源产生可观测信号，信号需被校准或控制风险，最终校准后的不确定性决定系统行为。核心观点是不确定性不应仅作为置信度数字评估，而应评估其是否能在证据不足、冲突、分布偏移或高风险的多模态证据下改善系统行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs的回答正确性依赖于视觉、文本、时间、声学、文档、图表或具身等多模态证据，其失败不仅是语言层面的——流畅的回答可能掩盖输入质量差、感知错误、接地弱、模态冲突、推理不稳定、分布偏移或问题本身不可回答等问题。现有综述大多聚焦于文本单模态不确定性或宽泛的MLLM能力，缺乏针对不确定性感知MLLM的系统性整理。

Method: 提出一个决策中心框架（decision-centered framework），将不确定性研究组织为三个层次：不确定性来源（sources）→ 可观测信号（signals）→ 风险校准与控制（calibration/control）→ 系统行为决策（system action）。综述涵盖词元/对数概率不确定性、语义分歧、扰动不稳定性、接地与归因分数、口头化置信度、验证器与评判器分数、共形预测、选择性回答、弃权、澄清、检索、自检和升级等研究方向。

Result: 将不确定性感知MLLM文献按照来源-信号-校准-行动框架系统化，并与文本单模态不确定性综述、通用MLLM综述、MLLM幻觉综述和安全导向综述进行了定位区分。

Conclusion: 提出开放问题：源感知分解（source-aware decomposition）、行动感知基准（action-aware benchmarks）、分布偏移下的校准、黑盒不确定性估计、更广模态覆盖、可复现报告、以人为中心的不确定性沟通。核心主张是不确定性评估应以其对行为改善的实际影响为标准。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly answer questions whose correctness depends on visual, textual, temporal, acoustic, document, chart, or embodied evidence. Their failures are therefore not only linguistic. A fluent answer may conceal poor input quality, a perceptual error, weak grounding, conflict between modalities, unstable reasoning, distribution shift, or a question that is not answerable from the supplied evidence. This survey organizes the literature on uncertainty-aware MLLMs around a decision-centered framework: uncertainty sources give rise to observable signals, signals must be calibrated or controlled for risk, and calibrated uncertainty should determine the system action. We review work on token and logit uncertainty, semantic disagreement, perturbation instability, grounding and attribution scores, verbalized confidence, verifier and judge scores, conformal prediction, selective answering, abstention, clarification, retrieval, self-checking, and escalation. The central argument is that uncertainty should not be evaluated only as a confidence number; it should be evaluated by whether it improves behavior under insufficient, conflicting, shifted, or high-risk multimodal evidence. We position this survey against text-only uncertainty and abstention surveys, broad MLLM surveys, MLLM hallucination surveys, and safety-oriented reviews. We conclude with open problems in source-aware decomposition, action-aware benchmarks, calibration under shift, black-box uncertainty estimation, broader modality coverage, reproducible reporting, and human-centered uncertainty communication.

</details>


### [96] [There is No Theoretical Curse of Multilinguality For Embedding Space Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17088)
*Niyati Bafna,Neha Verma,Vilém Zouhar,Philipp Koehn,David Yarowsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从理论上论证"多语言诅咒"并非嵌入空间结构的固有限制。作者形式化了"完美多语言性"的两个条件，并证明实现完美多语言性所需的最小维度仅随语言数量呈对数增长，表明嵌入空间结构本身不存在理论上的多语言诅咒，经验上的性能退化源于实际数据与训练条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言NLP的核心目标是用一个模型同时实现各语言优秀的单语性能与跨语言对齐。然而"多语言诅咒"现象（随语言覆盖增加，模型性能退化）威胁这一目标。本文想探究：多语言嵌入空间是否本质上无法实现完美多语言性，除非能力需求以不可接受的速度增长？

Method: 首先形式化"完美多语言性"的概念，将其凝练为两个多语言性条件；然后从理论上证明实现完美多语言性所需的最小嵌入维度仅随语言数量呈对数增长，从而在数学上排除了嵌入空间结构层面的"多语言诅咒"；最后通过小规模实证研究支持理论结论。

Result: 理论证明：完美多语言性所需的最小维度随语言数仅以对数规模增长，即嵌入空间结构不存在理论上的多语言诅咒。小规模实证研究为这一理论理解提供了佐证。

Conclusion: 经验中观察到的多语言诅咒并非嵌入空间的固有属性，而是源于现实世界数据与训练条件的限制。本文提供了关于多语言诅咒的首个理论性与内在视角，对理解该现象具有科学意义。

Abstract: A central goal of multilingual NLP is to achieve high monolingual performance per language and cross-lingual alignment for large-scale language coverage with a multilingual model. The curse of multilinguality describes the phenomenon of degradation in multilingual model performance as we increase language coverage, posing a threat to the above goal. This paper asks whether multilingual embedding spaces are inherently incapable of achieving perfect multilinguality without a prohibitive increase in required capacity. We first formalize the goal of "perfect multilinguality", embodied in two multilinguality conditions. We then prove that the minimum dimensionality required for perfect multilinguality grows only logarithmically in the number of languages. That is, we show that there is no theoretical curse of multilinguality for embedding space structure. This suggests that the empirical curse of multilinguality is a result of real world data and training conditions. We back this understanding with a small-scale empirical study. Our paper provides the first theoretical and intrinsic perspective on the curse of multilinguality, with implications for the scientific understanding of this phenomenon.

</details>


### [97] [A Glyph Is Not a Letter, a Token Is Not a Word, a Space Is Not a Space: What the Units of Voynichese Are Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17096)
*Liudmila Rozanova,Alexander Temerev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验了伏尼契手稿分析的三个隐含假设（字形是字母、字符串是单词、空白是词间距），通过对照匹配的散文、密码和伪文本语料及四开本重采样，发现三个假设均不成立。伏尼契文的秩序存在于词元边缘和渐变边界处，而非词元序列本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伏尼契手稿研究长期基于三个未经检验的假设（字形=字母、字符串=单词、空白=词间距），但这些假设从未被严格验证。作者希望用定量方法检验这些基本前提，而非继续在假设之上构建解释。

Method: 使用Zandbergen-Landini转写文本，配以匹配的散文、密码和伪文本对照组，采用四开本级重采样进行统计检验。测量了条件熵、词元间互信息、词边缘字形耦合、空白物理宽度（AUC 0.905）、以及学习单元对空白的跨越行为。同时引入已发表的伏尼契模拟密码和自引用文本生成器作为对照。

Result: 三项假设均被拒绝：字形规律性过强（条件熵2.7比特，低于拉丁语/意大利语/英语约3.5比特）；词元间预测仅占词元熵的不到1%（对照为2-10%）；不确定标记的空白行为类似词内连接点，物理上更窄且被学习单元跨越。模拟密码能重现部分特征（低熵、弱词序、替换攻击无效）但无法重现边缘字形耦合和开放的高hapax词汇（70%单例类型对41%和59-60%）。

Conclusion: 任何对伏尼契手稿的解释都必须"挣得"而非"假设"从字形、词元和分隔符到字母、单词和词间距的跃迁，本文提供的测量指标是进行这一论证的定量基础。

Abstract: The Voynich manuscript (Beinecke MS 408) is usually analysed on three unstated assumptions: that its glyphs are letters, that the strings between blanks are words, and that every blank is a word space. We test all three against the Zandbergen-Landini transliteration with matched prose, cipher, and pseudo-text controls and quire-level resampling. None holds, and the failures share a shape: the order in Voynichese sits at the edges of tokens and at graded boundaries between them, not in the succession of tokens themselves. Glyph regularity is too strong for one-to-one substitution of any tested plaintext (conditional entropy 2.7 bits against about 3.5 for Latin, Italian, and English) and resolves instead onto a quire-stable scale of recurrent multi-symbol units.
